[EVENT: YEM78078]
Date: 2023-01-01
Country: Yemen | Location: Al Hawtah
Type: Protests > Protest with intervention | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Yemen) [Protesters]
Actor2: Military Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council - STC Support and Reinforcement Brigades [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Al Masdar; Nabd al Sharea; Undisclosed Source; Yemen Data Project [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 January 2023, hundreds of protesters from Al Madaribah Wa Al Arah and Radfan areas (including Al Habilayn, Halimayn, Habil Jabr, and Al Milah) gathered in Al Hawtah (Lahij) to denounce corrupt leaders and demand that STC forces hand over fighters from the 5th Support and Reinforcement Brigade who killed a man in early December. STC Support and Reinforcement and Security Belt forces deployed to the entrances of Al Hawtah and arrested several protesters. Troops from the 5th STC Support and Reinforcement Brigade also cut a road in the area to prevent buses of protesters from reaching Al Hawtah city.

---

[EVENT: YEM78072]
Date: 2023-01-01
Country: Yemen | Location: Mudiyah
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Yemen) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Aden al Ghad; Undisclosed Source [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 January 2023, protesters held a large march in the streets of Mudiyah (Mudiyah, Abyan) to demand the dismissal of the director-general of the district who stands accused of corruption involving illegal financial deductions from the salaries of employees in the health and educational sectors. Other figures in the local authority have also been accused of involvement. Protesters vowed to escalate the situation if their demands are not met.

[EVENT: YEM78249]
Date: 2023-01-10
Country: Yemen | Location: Lawdar
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Police Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council - STC Security Belt Forces [State forces]
Actor2: Southern Resistance [Political militia]
Fatalities: 5
Sources: Al Mahrah Post; Al Masdar; Crater Sky; Undisclosed Source; Xinhua; Yemen Data Project; Yemen Press Agency [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 10 January 2023, STC Security Belt Forces clashed with Southern Resistance Forces from the Al Awadhil tribe in Lawdar (Lawdar, Abyan). The clashes started following a dispute at the market in the city, when a commander in the Security Belt's Emergency Unit opened fire on Southern Resistance forces, killing and wounding a number of soldiers and civilians. Later that day, fighting occurred between Security Belt forces and units affiliated with the Southern Resistance after the latter seized control of the Security Belt headquarters in the city, resulting in a number of deaths and injuries on both sides. The Southern Resistance eventually withdrew from the Security Belt headquarters after holding it for several hours following tribal mediation and after IRG police forces commanded by Abyan security director Brigadier General Al-Kazmi intervened. The initial shooting and eventual clashes resulted in at least 5 fatalities and other injuries on both sides and among civilians.

[EVENT: YEM78539]
Date: 2023-01-24
Country: Yemen | Location: Judaydah
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Yemen) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Ansar Allah; Undisclosed Source [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 24 January 2023, residents from Al Jubah district held a large Houthi-sponsored demonstration in the district (coded to district center Judaydah) (Marib) to denounce Western attitudes towards Islam and Muslims following the burning of the Quran in Sweden and the Netherlands. Under the slogan, 'The Anger of the People of Faith Against Those Who Burned the Quran', the protesters urged the boycott of Swedish, along with American and what was termed 'Zionist' goods. Residents of Jabal Murad and Rahabah districts also attended the Al-Jubah protest. The protest was in response to Swedish police authorizing a far-right activist to burn the Quran outside the Turkish embassy in Stockholm in reaction to Turkey blocking Sweden's entry into the NATO alliance. Days later, a far-right Dutch activist ripped pages from a Quran and stepped on them outside the Dutch parliament.

The reason behind the incident was not reported. The source reported a strike of the hospital staff on 29 January that was a response to the attack but when the attack took place is unclear.
